# List 25

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sunder</b></td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disassociate</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分离，使脱离关系</td><td>disassociate oneself from<br>与…划清界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party moved to disassociate itself from the remarks.  该党着手与那些言论划清界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disunite</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分裂，使不团结</td><td>disunite the party<br>使党派分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax proposal disunited a coalition that had held for years.  这项税收提案使维持多年的联盟分裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 part</td><td>/ˈpɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>part the heavy curtains<br>分开厚重的窗帘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She parted the heavy curtains and let the morning light in.  她分开厚重的窗帘，让晨光照进来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rend</td><td>/rend/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撕裂，猛拉</td><td>rend the cloth in two<br>把布撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry protester tried to rend the banner into two ragged halves.  那名愤怒的抗议者试图把横幅撕成破烂的两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rive</td><td>/raɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>撕开</td><td>rive the log apart<br>把木头劈开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lightning can rive a tall oak straight down the middle of its trunk.  闪电能把高大的橡树从树干正中劈开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sever</td><td>/ˈsevər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切断，断绝</td><td>sever all ties<br>断绝一切关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company severed all ties with its former supplier.  公司与前供应商断绝了一切关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unyoke</del></td><td>/ʌnˈjoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开轭；使分离，解脱</td><td>unyoke the oxen<br>给牛解轭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At dusk the workers unyoked the oxen and led them to water.  黄昏时工人给牛解开轭，牵它们去饮水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bond</td><td>/ˈbɑːnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使结合，联结</td><td>bond the layers together<br>把各层黏合起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glue bonds metal to glass in seconds.  这种胶数秒内就能把金属与玻璃黏合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yoke</td><td>/joʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使结合，联结；给…上轭</td><td>yoke two oxen together<br>把两头牛套在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer yoked two oxen together to pull the heavy plow.  农夫把两头牛套在一起拉那沉重的犁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supercilious</b></td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arrogant</td><td>/ˈærəɡənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自大的</td><td>an arrogant manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arrogant remarks cost him the respect of his colleagues.  他傲慢的言论使他失去了同事的尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assumptive</del></td><td>/əˈsʌmptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自以为是的；假定的</td><td>an assumptive attitude<br>傲慢的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His assumptive manner annoyed everyone at the negotiating table.  他傲慢的态度让谈判桌上的人都很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumptious</td><td>/ˈbʌmpʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横傲慢的，自以为是的</td><td>a bumptious young man<br>自以为是的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her bumptious manner annoyed the older members of the committee.  她自以为是的态度让委员会里年长的成员很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haughty</td><td>/ˈhɔːti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，目中无人的</td><td>a haughty manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the waiter a haughty look and said nothing.  她傲慢地看了服务员一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>an imperious tone<br>专横的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her imperious manner made junior colleagues afraid to disagree.  她专横的态度让年轻同事不敢有异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pompous</td><td>/ˈpɑːmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的；傲慢的</td><td>a pompous speech<br>浮夸的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pompous speech bored everyone in the crowded town hall.  那场浮夸的演讲让拥挤的市政厅里每个人都厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumptuous</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冒失的，放肆的</td><td>presumptuous of you<br>你太放肆了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems presumptuous to criticize a book you have not read.  批评一本没读过的书，似乎太放肆了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overbearing</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈberɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横傲慢的</td><td>an overbearing boss<br>专横傲慢的老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His overbearing manner made junior colleagues afraid to voice any objection.  他专横的作风让年轻同事不敢提出任何反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superior</td><td>/suːˈpɪriər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>更好的，占优势的；上级</td><td>superior to the rest<br>优于其余各方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This model is clearly superior to the older version.  这个型号明显优于旧版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>superficial</b></td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 facile</td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfunctory</td><td>/pərˈfʌŋktəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>敷衍的，草率的</td><td>a perfunctory nod<br>敷衍的点头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the report a perfunctory glance and signed it immediately.  他敷衍地扫了一眼报告就立刻签了字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shallow</td><td>/ˈʃæloʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅薄的，肤浅的；浅的</td><td>shallow water<br>浅水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arguments are shallow and easy to refute.  他的论点浅薄，很容易反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sketchy</td><td>/ˈsketʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗略的，不详尽的</td><td>only sketchy details available<br>只有粗略的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early reports about the fire were sketchy and often wrong.  关于火灾的早期报道很粗略，还常有错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skin-deep</td><td>/ˌskɪn ˈdiːp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>beauty is only skin-deep<br>美貌只是表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His interest in the subject turned out to be skin-deep.  他对这个题目的兴趣原来只是浮于表面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>superfluous</b></td><td>/suːˈpɜːrfluəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多余的，过剩的</td><td>superfluous detail<br>多余的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut every superfluous detail from the opening chapter.  编辑删掉了开篇一章中所有多余的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excessive</td><td>/ɪkˈsesɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>excessive drinking<br>过量饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors warned him about his excessive salt intake.  医生警告他盐摄入过量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extra</td><td>/ˈekstrə/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>额外的，多余的</td><td>an extra copy<br>多出的一份副本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We packed extra blankets in case the night turned cold.  我们多带了几条毯子，以防夜里变冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redundant</td><td>/rɪˈdʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多余的，冗余的</td><td>redundant information<br>多余的信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut several paragraphs of redundant information from the report.  编辑从报告中删去了几段多余的信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surplus</td><td>/ˈsɜːrpləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>过剩，剩余</td><td>a grain surplus<br>粮食过剩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country exported its grain surplus to neighboring markets.  该国把过剩的粮食出口到邻国市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deficient</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>deficient in vitamins<br>缺乏维生素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diet was deficient in iron and essential vitamins.  这种饮食缺乏铁和必需的维生素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inadequate</td><td>/ɪnˈædəkwət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不充足的，不胜任的</td><td>inadequate funding<br>资金不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lighting in the room is completely inadequate.  房间的照明完全不够。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insufficient</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，不够的</td><td>insufficient evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evidence was insufficient to convict him of the crime.  证据不足，无法判定他有罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 short</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短缺的，不足的</td><td>short of money<br>缺钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are short of money until the end of the month.  到月底之前我们都缺钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 skimpy</td><td>/ˈskɪmpi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，少得可怜的</td><td>a skimpy portion of meat<br>少得可怜的一份肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report gave only skimpy evidence for its bold claims.  报告只给出很少的证据来支持大胆的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sparse</td><td>/spɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀疏的，稀少的</td><td>sparse vegetation<br>稀疏的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vegetation grows sparse as the trail climbs above the tree line.  小径爬上林线以上后，植被变得稀疏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stagnant</b></td><td>/ˈstæɡnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>停滞不前的</td><td>a stagnant economy<br>停滞的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The economy stayed stagnant for a decade despite repeated government reforms.  尽管政府反复改革，经济十年停滞不前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supine</b></td><td>/ˈsuːpaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仰卧的；消极的，漠不关心的</td><td>supine acceptance of injustice<br>对不公的消极接受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The legislature's supine acceptance of injustice angered many ordinary citizens.  立法机关对不公的消极接受激怒了许多普通市民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passive</td><td>/ˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被动的，消极的</td><td>a passive observer<br>被动的旁观者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed passive while others argued fiercely about the plan.  别人激烈争论方案时，他始终无所作为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dormant</td><td>/ˈdɔːrmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，不活跃的</td><td>a dormant volcano<br>休眠火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can stay dormant in the body for years.  这种病毒可在体内潜伏多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigilant</td><td>/ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警醒的，警惕的</td><td>remain vigilant<br>保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards must remain vigilant throughout the long and quiet night shift.  守卫在漫长安静的夜班中必须始终保持警惕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supple</b></td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bendy</td><td>/ˈbendi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔韧的，易弯曲的</td><td>a bendy plastic straw<br>可弯的塑料吸管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toy is made of a bendy rubber that never cracks.  这个玩具由永不裂开的柔韧橡胶制成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limber</td><td>/ˈlɪmbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软灵活的</td><td>stay limber and loose<br>保持柔软放松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dancers stretch every morning to stay limber and avoid painful injuries.  舞者每天早上拉伸，以保持柔软并避免疼痛的伤病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lissome</del></td><td>/ˈlɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的；敏捷的，轻盈的</td><td>a lissome dancer<br>身姿柔软的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lissome dancer moved across the stage as if she were weightless.  那位身姿柔软的舞者仿佛毫无重量地掠过舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lithe</td><td>/laɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，轻盈的；柔软的</td><td>a lithe young cat<br>轻盈的小猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was lithe enough to slip through the narrow gap.  她身形轻盈，足以从窄缝中溜过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lithesome</del></td><td>/ˈlaɪðsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的，轻盈敏捷的</td><td>lithesome movements<br>轻盈的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lithesome gymnast crossed the beam without a wobble.  这位轻盈的体操运动员走过平衡木毫无摇晃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supplant</b></td><td>/səˈplænt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排挤，篡夺…的位置</td><td>supplant the old leader<br>取代原来的领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap digital cameras quickly supplanted film in the consumer market.  廉价数码相机迅速在消费市场取代了胶片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 displace</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取代，使离开原位</td><td>displace local workers<br>取代本地工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Automation displaced hundreds of workers at the old assembly plant.  自动化取代了那家老装配厂的数百名工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 displant</del></td><td>/dɪsˈplænt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取代；使迁离故土</td><td>displant the native tribes<br>使原住部落迁离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Settlers gradually displanted the tribes who had farmed the valley.  移民逐渐使世代耕种这座山谷的部落迁离故土。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substitute</td><td>/ˈsʌbstɪtuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>取代者，替代品</td><td>a poor substitute for milk<br>牛奶的劣质替代品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Powdered milk is a poor substitute for the fresh kind.  奶粉是鲜奶的劣质替代品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supersede</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取代，替代</td><td>supersede the old rules<br>取代旧规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New safety regulations will supersede the old rules next January.  新的安全条例将于明年一月取代旧规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut out</td><td>/kʌt ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取代（对手）；删去</td><td>cut out a rival<br>挤掉竞争对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cut out his rival and won the client's contract himself.  他挤掉了对手，自己赢得了客户的合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supplement</b></td><td>/ˈsʌplɪmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>增补，补充</td><td>supplement one's income<br>补充收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took an evening job to supplement his modest teaching income.  他找了份晚间工作，以补充微薄的教书收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 addendum</td><td>/əˈdendəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>附录，补遗</td><td>an addendum to the contract<br>合同的附加条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short addendum to the contract listed the two new deadlines.  合同后的一份简短附录列出了两个新的截止日期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 addition</td><td>/əˈdɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>增加，添加物</td><td>a welcome addition<br>受欢迎的新增内容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new wing is a welcome addition to the crowded library.  新翼楼是拥挤图书馆一个受欢迎的增建。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expansion</td><td>/ɪkˈspænʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>扩充，扩展</td><td>rapid business expansion<br>业务快速扩张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid expansion left the young company badly short of cash.  快速扩张使这家年轻公司严重缺乏现金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 increment</td><td>/ˈɪnkrəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>增量，增加</td><td>a small annual increment<br>小幅年度增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Salaries rise by a small increment every January.  工资每年一月小幅增长一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proliferation</td><td>/prəˌlɪfəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>激增，扩散</td><td>the proliferation of weapons<br>武器扩散</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proliferation of cheap sensors changed how farmers water crops.  廉价传感器的激增改变了农民灌溉作物的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 step-up</td><td>/ˈstɛp ʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>增加，加强</td><td>a step-up in production<br>产量的提升</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory announced a sharp step-up in weekend production.  工厂宣布周末产量大幅提升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supplicate</b></td><td>/ˈsʌplɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>恳求，乞求</td><td>supplicate for forgiveness<br>乞求宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoner knelt to supplicate for forgiveness before the military tribunal.  囚犯跪下，在军事法庭前乞求宽恕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beseech</td><td>/bɪˈsiːtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，哀求</td><td>beseech him for mercy<br>恳求他宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners beseeched the governor for mercy in a long letter.  囚犯们在一封长信中恳求州长开恩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjure</td><td>/kənˈdʒʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，央求；使浮现于脑海</td><td>conjure him to stay<br>恳求他留下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She conjured him to stay until the doctor finally arrived.  她恳求他留下，直到医生终于赶来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entreat</td><td>/ɪnˈtriːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求</td><td>entreat her to return<br>恳求她回来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She entreated him to return before their mother learned of the quarrel.  她恳求他在母亲得知这场争吵之前回来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 impetrate</del></td><td>/ˈɪmpɪtreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，祈求得到</td><td>impetrate divine mercy<br>祈求神的宽恕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gathered to impetrate rain from the heavens.  村民聚集起来向上天祈求降雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implore</td><td>/ˌɪmˈplɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恳求，哀求</td><td>implore for help<br>苦苦求助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She implored the judge to give her brother another chance.  她恳求法官再给她弟弟一次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solicit</td><td>/səˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>请求，征求；招揽</td><td>solicit donations<br>募集捐款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity solicited funds from local businesses all winter.  该慈善机构整个冬天向本地企业募捐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plead to</td><td>/pliːd tuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>向…恳求，请求</td><td>plead to the judge<br>向法官恳求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoner pleaded to the judge for a lighter sentence.  囚犯向法官恳求减轻判刑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appeal to</td><td>/əˈpiːl tuː/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>向…呼吁，恳求</td><td>appeal to the public<br>向公众呼吁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity appealed to donors for urgent help after the flood.  洪灾后慈善机构向捐赠者紧急求助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demand</td><td>/dɪˈmænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强烈要求；需求</td><td>demand an apology<br>要求道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry customers demanded an apology from the store manager.  愤怒的顾客要求店长道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>supposition</b></td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>猜想，推测</td><td>based on supposition<br>基于猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conclusion rests on supposition rather than on hard evidence.  他们的结论基于猜测，而非确凿证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hypothesis</td><td>/haɪˈpɑːθəsəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>假设，假说</td><td>test a hypothesis<br>检验一个假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three experiments were designed to test her bold new hypothesis.  设计了三个实验来检验她大胆的新假设。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proposition</td><td>/ˌprɑːpəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>命题；主张，提议</td><td>an attractive proposition<br>有吸引力的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moving abroad for work seemed an attractive proposition at first.  出国工作起初似乎是个有吸引力的提议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surmise</td><td>/sərˈmaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（根据不足的）推测，揣测</td><td>a mere surmise<br>只是一种揣测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without documents, his account is a mere surmise about what happened.  没有文件，他的说法只是对当时情形的揣测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 premise</td><td>/ˈpremɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>前提，假定</td><td>a false premise<br>错误的前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole plan rests on a false premise about demand.  整个计划建立在一个关于需求的错误前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumption</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冒失无礼；假定</td><td>forgive my presumption<br>请原谅我的冒失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was sheer presumption to speak for the whole committee.  代表整个委员会发言纯属冒失无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certainty</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtənti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确定，必然；确信</td><td>with absolute certainty<br>绝对确定地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one can predict the outcome with absolute certainty.  没人能绝对确定地预测结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>suppress</b></td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clamp down on</td><td>/klæmp ˈdaʊn ɑn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>严厉打击，压制</td><td>clamp down on speeding<br>严查超速</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city clamped down on illegal parking near the school gates.  市里严厉整治校门附近的违章停车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crack down on</td><td>/kræk ˈdaʊn ɑn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>严厉打击，取缔</td><td>crack down on fraud<br>严打欺诈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regulators finally cracked down on the unlicensed lending apps.  监管部门终于取缔了这些无照放贷软件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 put down</td><td>/pʊt ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>记下，写下</td><td>put down a few notes<br>记下几条笔记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He put down the address before he forgot it.  他趁没忘先记下了地址。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quash</td><td>/kwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；镇压，阻止</td><td>quash a conviction<br>撤销有罪判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeal court quashed his conviction last week.  上诉法院上周撤销了对他的定罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repress</td><td>/rɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压；阻止（正常的表达、活动或发展等）</td><td>repress a smile<br>忍住笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She struggled to repress a smile during the solemn ceremony.  在庄严的仪式上她努力忍住笑意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silence</td><td>/ˈsaɪləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>寂静，沉默；使沉默</td><td>break the silence<br>打破沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long silence followed his surprising announcement to the class.  他向全班宣布这惊人消息后是长久的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slap down</td><td>/slæp ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>压制，严词驳斥</td><td>slap down a suggestion<br>断然否决建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chairman slapped down every suggestion from the younger staff.  主席把年轻员工的每个建议都断然压了下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snuff out</td><td>/snʌf ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>扑灭；终止，毁灭</td><td>snuff out a candle<br>掐灭蜡烛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single storm can snuff out years of careful planting.  一场风暴就能毁掉多年的精心种植。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squash</td><td>/ˈskwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压，压制；压扁</td><td>squash the rumor<br>压下谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly squashed the rumor about layoffs.  公司迅速压下了关于裁员的谣言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squelch</td><td>/skweltʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制，镇压（运动）</td><td>squelch the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops moved in quickly to squelch the rebellion in the province.  军队迅速开进，镇压该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdue</td><td>/səbˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使顺从；征服</td><td>subdue the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government forces moved south to subdue the rebellion within a week.  政府军南下，一周内镇压了叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 censor</td><td>/ˈsensər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>审查删改；审查员</td><td>censor the news<br>审查新闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials censored the news before it reached the public.  官员在新闻传到公众之前进行了审查删改。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clean</td><td>/ˈkliːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干净的；打扫，清洗</td><td>a clean white shirt<br>干净的白衬衫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep the wound clean and change the bandage every day.  保持伤口干净，每天更换绷带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expurgate</del></td><td>/ˈekspərɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>净化（书等），删去（不当处）</td><td>expurgate a text<br>删改文本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors expurgated several passages before the book reached young readers.  编辑在这本书交到小读者手中之前删去了几个段落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>surcharge</b></td><td>/ˈsɜːrtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>多收费；附加费</td><td>pay a fuel surcharge<br>支付燃油附加费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline added a fuel surcharge to every international ticket this month.  航空公司本月给每张国际机票加了燃油附加费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gouge</td><td>/ˈɡaʊdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>敲竹杠，索价过高</td><td>gouge the customers<br>向顾客乱收高价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hotels near the stadium gouge visitors on every game weekend.  体育场附近的旅馆每个比赛周末都向游客乱收高价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soak</td><td>/soʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使…湿透</td><td>soak the beans overnight<br>把豆子浸泡一夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soak the dried beans overnight before cooking them the next morning.  把干豆浸泡一夜，第二天早上再煮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sting</td><td>/ˈstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>刺痛；蜇，刺</td><td>the sting of the wound<br>伤口的刺痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The alcohol caused a fierce sting on the cut.  酒精让伤口一阵剧烈刺痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>surfeit</b></td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overabundance</td><td>/ˌoʊvərəˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>过多，过剩</td><td>an overabundance of caution<br>过度的谨慎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An overabundance of rain ruined most of the wheat crop.  雨水过多毁掉了大部分小麦收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overage</td><td>/ˈoʊvərɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>过剩量，超出额</td><td>a large overage of stock<br>大量的库存过剩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The warehouse reported a costly overage of unsold winter coats.  仓库报告了一批代价高昂的滞销冬衣过剩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overflow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈfloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>溢出，泛滥</td><td>overflow the river banks<br>河水漫过堤岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Melting snow made the river overflow its banks last spring.  去年春天融雪使河水漫过堤岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plethora</td><td>/ˈpleθərə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>过量，过剩</td><td>a plethora of options<br>过多的选项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The menu offers a plethora of options that confuse first time visitors.  菜单提供过多的选项，让初次光顾的客人不知所选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redundancy</td><td>/rɪˈdʌndənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>多余，过剩</td><td>unnecessary redundancy<br>不必要的冗余</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut the redundancy from every paragraph of the draft.  编辑删去了草稿中每一段的冗余内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 superfluity</del></td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfluːəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>过剩，多余之物</td><td>a superfluity of detail<br>过多的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report drowned its argument in a superfluity of statistics.  这份报告用过多的统计数据淹没了自己的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surplus</td><td>/ˈsɜːrpləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>过剩，剩余</td><td>a grain surplus<br>粮食过剩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country exported its grain surplus to neighboring markets.  该国把过剩的粮食出口到邻国市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deficiency</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃnsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏，不足</td><td>a vitamin deficiency<br>维生素缺乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A serious iron deficiency left her tired and short of breath.  严重缺铁使她疲倦、气短。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deprivation</td><td>/ˌdeprɪˈveɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剥夺；匮乏</td><td>sleep deprivation<br>睡眠剥夺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sleep deprivation left the young doctor exhausted and irritable.  多年的睡眠剥夺让这位年轻医生疲惫易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insufficient supply</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənt səˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>an insufficient supply of water<br>水供应不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insufficient supply of vaccines delayed the clinic's program by weeks.  疫苗供应不足使诊所的计划推迟了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 famish</del></td><td>/ˈfæmɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使饥饿，使饿得难受</td><td>famish the besieged city<br>使被围城市陷入饥饿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long siege famished the townspeople before help finally arrived.  漫长的围城使镇民饥饿难耐，援助才终于到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 starve</td><td>/ˈstɑːrv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使饥饿，饿死</td><td>starve to death<br>饿死</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The siege starved the town for nearly two months.  围城让这座城镇饥饿了近两个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>surrender</b></td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cede</td><td>/siːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，割让</td><td>cede territory<br>割让领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated nation was forced to cede two provinces.  战败国被迫割让两个省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relinquish</td><td>/rɪˈlɪŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃（权力、职位）</td><td>relinquish control<br>放弃控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to relinquish control of the family business.  她拒绝放弃对家族企业的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resign</td><td>/rɪˈzaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辞职，放弃（职位）</td><td>resign from office<br>辞去公职</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appropriate</td><td>/əˈproʊprieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>私自挪用；适当的</td><td>appropriate public funds<br>挪用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials were accused of appropriating public money for private use.  官员们被指控将公款挪为私用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>surreptitious</b></td><td>/ˌsɜːrəpˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偷偷摸摸的，保密的</td><td>a surreptitious glance<br>偷偷的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a surreptitious glance at his watch during the meeting.  会上他偷偷看了一眼手表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backstairs</td><td>/ˈbækˌsterz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的，暗中进行的</td><td>backstairs negotiations<br>暗中的谈判</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clandestine</td><td>/klænˈdestɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的，暗中的</td><td>a clandestine meeting<br>秘密的会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They arranged a clandestine meeting in an empty parking garage.  他们在一个空停车场安排了秘密会面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covert</td><td>/ˈkoʊvərt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐蔽的，秘密的</td><td>covert operations<br>秘密行动</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 furtive</td><td>/ˈfɜːrtɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>鬼鬼祟祟的；秘密的</td><td>a furtive glance<br>偷偷的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a furtive glance at the answers on her desk.  他偷偷瞥了一眼她桌上的答案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 privy</td><td>/ˈprɪvi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>私下知情的；秘密的</td><td>privy to the secret plan<br>私下知晓密谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two aides were privy to the president's real schedule.  只有两名助手私下知道总统真正的行程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underground</td><td>/ˈʌndərɡraʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>秘密的；地下的</td><td>an underground movement<br>地下运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group published an underground newspaper during the occupation.  该团体在占领期间出版了一份地下报纸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underhanded</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈhændɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，暗中进行的</td><td>underhanded tactics<br>暗中使用的手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They won the contract through underhanded tactics.  他们靠不正当手段赢得了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 behind-the-scenes</td><td>/bɪˌhaɪnd ðə ˈsiːnz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>幕后的，秘密进行的</td><td>behind-the-scenes negotiations<br>幕后谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of behind-the-scenes negotiations produced the surprise agreement.  数月的幕后谈判促成了这份令人意外的协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 barefaced</td><td>/ˈberfeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然的，厚颜无耻的</td><td>a barefaced lie<br>明目张胆的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He told a barefaced lie to the committee without blinking.  他毫不眨眼地对委员会撒了个明目张胆的谎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aboveboard</td><td>/əˈbʌvbɔːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无欺诈的，光明正大的</td><td>open and aboveboard<br>公开而光明正大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole deal was entirely open and aboveboard.  整笔交易完全是光明正大的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overt</td><td>/oʊˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公开的</td><td>overt hostility<br>公开的敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The policy met overt hostility from local business owners.  该政策遭到当地商户的公开敌视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 public</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>公开的；公众的</td><td>make it public<br>予以公开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made its findings public earlier this month.  公司本月早些时候公开了调查结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>susceptible</b></td><td>/səˈseptəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受影响的；易感染的</td><td>susceptible to infection<br>易受感染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children are especially susceptible to infection during the cold months.  幼儿在寒冷的月份里尤其容易受到感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endangered</td><td>/ɪnˈdeɪndʒərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>处于危险中的，濒危的</td><td>an endangered species<br>濒危物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising temperatures have left many mountain bird species endangered.  气温上升使许多山地鸟类濒临危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exposed</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无遮蔽的，易受攻击的</td><td>exposed to attack<br>易受攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The camp sat on an exposed ridge with no shelter from wind.  营地位于毫无避风处的裸露山脊上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subject</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>取决于（其他因素）的，受制于</td><td>subject to approval<br>须经批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The entire plan is subject to approval by the city council.  整个方案须经市议会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vulnerable</td><td>/ˈvʌlnərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，脆弱的</td><td>vulnerable to attack<br>易受攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wooden houses on the ridge are especially vulnerable to summer fires.  山脊上的木屋在夏季尤其容易受火灾侵袭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 insusceptible</del></td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈseptəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不易受影响的</td><td>insusceptible to flattery<br>不为奉承所动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old general seemed insusceptible to fear of any kind.  这位老将军似乎不受任何恐惧的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invulnerable</td><td>/ɪnˈvʌlnərəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不会受伤的，无懈可击的</td><td>invulnerable to attack<br>不受攻击伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No fortress is truly invulnerable to a long siege.  没有哪座堡垒真能经受长期围攻而不受损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unexposed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈspoʊzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未暴露的，未受影响的</td><td>unexposed to the virus<br>未接触过病毒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children unexposed to the virus were vaccinated first.  未接触过病毒的儿童被优先接种。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unsusceptible</del></td><td>/ˌʌnsəˈsɛptəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不易受影响的，免疫的</td><td>unsusceptible to flattery<br>不吃奉承那一套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old judge seemed entirely unsusceptible to flattery or pressure.  这位老法官似乎完全不受奉承或压力的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>suspend</b></td><td>/səˈspend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暂停，中止</td><td>suspend the hearing<br>中止听证会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials had to suspend the search because of the storm.  由于暴风雨，官员们不得不中止搜救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prorogue</td><td>/proʊˈroʊɡ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使休会，中止议会会期</td><td>prorogue parliament<br>使议会休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prime minister asked the queen to prorogue parliament.  首相请求女王让议会休会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recess</td><td>/rɪˈses/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>休会，休庭；课间休息</td><td>take a short recess<br>短暂休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee will recess for lunch and resume at two.  委员会将休会吃午饭，两点复会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invoke</td><td>/ɪnˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>援引；引起，造成</td><td>invoke widespread anger<br>引发广泛的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clumsy apology only invoked more anger among the injured workers.  拙劣的道歉只是在受伤的工人中引发了更多愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>suture</b></td><td>/ˈsuːtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>缝合</td><td>suture the deep wound<br>缝合深创口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon sutured the deep wound with remarkable speed and care.  外科医生极其迅速细致地缝合了深创口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incision</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>切口，切开</td><td>a small surgical incision<br>小的手术切口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon closed the small incision with three neat stitches.  医生用三道整齐的针脚缝合了那个小切口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 avulse</del></td><td>/əˈvʌls/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撕脱，猛力撕开</td><td>avulse the tendon<br>撕脱肌腱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The accident avulsed a tendon from the athlete's right heel.  这场事故把运动员右脚跟的一根肌腱撕脱了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>svelte</b></td><td>/svelt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（女人）体态苗条的，优雅的</td><td>a svelte silhouette<br>苗条优雅的身影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her svelte silhouette drew admiring glances across the crowded ballroom.  她苗条优雅的身影在拥挤的舞厅里引来赞赏的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bony</td><td>/ˈboʊni/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦骨嶙峋的，骨头突出的</td><td>bony shoulders<br>瘦削的肩膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bony wrists stuck out from sleeves that were far too short.  他瘦骨嶙峋的手腕从太短的袖子里露出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lean</td><td>/ˈliːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦的，精瘦的</td><td>lean and muscular<br>精瘦而结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of running left him lean and quick.  多年跑步让他精瘦而敏捷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skinny</td><td>/ˈskɪni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极瘦的，瘦得难看的</td><td>a skinny teenager<br>瘦弱的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skinny boy could barely lift the heavy water bucket.  那个瘦弱的男孩几乎提不起沉重的水桶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slender</td><td>/ˈslendər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苗条的，细长的</td><td>a slender waist<br>纤细的腰身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She held the flower by its slender stem.  她捏着那朵花细长的茎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slim</td><td>/ˈslɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微小的，微薄的；苗条的</td><td>a slim chance of success<br>极小的成功机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We have only a slim chance of catching the last train.  我们赶上末班车的机会很小。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plump</td><td>/plʌmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>丰满的，圆胖的；选定，支持</td><td>plump for the plan<br>选定支持这项计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the villagers plumped for the cheaper of the two plans.  大多数村民选了两个方案中较便宜的那个。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 corpulent</td><td>/ˈkɔːrpjələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥胖的，臃肿的</td><td>a corpulent gentleman<br>肥胖的绅士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The corpulent merchant filled the narrow doorway completely.  那位肥胖的商人把窄门堵得满满的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paunchy and awkward</td><td>/ˈpɔːntʃi ənd ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大腹便便且笨拙的</td><td>look paunchy and awkward<br>显得肥胖笨拙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the tight uniform he looked paunchy and awkward on stage.  穿着紧身制服，他在台上显得大腹便便又笨拙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>swagger</b></td><td>/ˈswæɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>趾高气扬地走；自夸</td><td>swagger about one's success<br>炫耀自己的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swaggered into the room after winning the bowling championship.  赢得保龄球锦标赛后，他大摇大摆地走进房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brag</td><td>/ˈbræɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>自夸，吹牛</td><td>brag about one's wealth<br>炫耀自己的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bragged about his new car until everyone stopped listening.  他一直吹嘘自己的新车，直到大家都不再听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vapor</td><td>/ˈveɪpər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>说大话，吹牛</td><td>vapor about past victories<br>吹嘘往日胜绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old colonel vapored about battles he had never fought.  那位老上校吹嘘他从未参加过的战役。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vaunt</del></td><td>/vɔːnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>吹嘘</td><td>vaunt one's achievements<br>夸耀自己的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He vaunted his prizes until even his friends stopped listening.  他不停吹嘘自己的奖项，连朋友都不再听了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gasconade</td><td>/ˌɡæskəˈneɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>夸口，吹牛</td><td>empty gasconade<br>空洞的吹牛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was pure gasconade with no facts behind it.  他的演讲纯属吹牛，背后毫无事实依据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>swear</b></td><td>/swer/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>宣誓；咒骂</td><td>swear to tell the truth<br>宣誓说出真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each witness must swear to tell the truth before testifying.  每位证人作证前必须宣誓说出真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blaspheme</td><td>/blæsˈfim/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>亵渎，辱骂（神圣之物）</td><td>blaspheme against God<br>亵渎神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that century anyone who blasphemed publicly risked imprisonment.  在那个世纪，公开亵渎神灵者可能被监禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curse</td><td>/ˈkɜːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>诅咒，咒骂</td><td>curse one's enemies<br>诅咒自己的敌人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman cursed her enemies in a trembling voice.  老妇人用颤抖的声音诅咒她的敌人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cuss</td><td>/ˈkʌs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>咒骂，说粗话</td><td>cuss at the driver<br>咒骂司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cussed loudly when the hammer landed on his thumb.  锤子砸到拇指时他大声咒骂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attest</td><td>/əˈtest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，为…作证</td><td>attest to his good character<br>证明他品行良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors will attest to his good character in court.  几位邻居将在法庭上证明他品行良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depose</td><td>/dɪˈpoʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>宣誓作证；废黜，罢免</td><td>depose the aging king<br>废黜年迈的国王</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebel officers deposed the president and installed a temporary ruling council.  叛军军官废黜了总统，成立了临时统治委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covenant</td><td>/ˈkʌvənənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>契约，盟约</td><td>a solemn covenant<br>庄严的盟约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two families entered a covenant to share the land.  两家订立盟约共用这块土地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pledge</td><td>/ˈpledʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>发誓，郑重承诺</td><td>pledge one's support<br>承诺给予支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both parties pledged their support for the flood relief fund.  两党都承诺支持这项洪灾救助基金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vow</td><td>/ˈvaʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>起誓，立誓</td><td>vow never to return<br>发誓绝不回来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She vowed never to set foot in that town again.  她发誓再也不踏进那个镇子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sweltering</b></td><td>/ˈsweltərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的</td><td>a sweltering afternoon<br>酷热的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed indoors through the sweltering afternoon with the fans running.  酷热的午后我们开着风扇待在屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boiling</td><td>/ˈbɔɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沸腾的；情绪激昂的</td><td>a boiling pot of water<br>一锅沸水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dropped the noodles into the boiling water and stirred once.  她把面条下进沸水里，搅了一下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scorching</td><td>/ˈskɔːrtʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a scorching summer day<br>酷热的夏日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crossed the desert under a scorching midday sun.  他们在酷热的正午阳光下穿越沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 searing</td><td>/ˈsɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灼热的；尖锐的</td><td>searing heat<br>灼热的高温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The searing heat of the furnace could be felt outside.  炉子的灼热在外面都能感觉到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sultry</td><td>/ˈsʌltri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷热的；性感撩人的</td><td>a sultry evening<br>闷热的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sultry air made sleeping almost impossible that night.  闷热的空气让那晚几乎无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torrid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiery</td><td>/ˈfaɪəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；火热的</td><td>the fiery desert sun<br>酷热的沙漠太阳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fiery afternoon sun drove every worker into the shade.  酷热的午后阳光把每个工人都赶进了阴凉处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 algid</del></td><td>/ˈældʒɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的，冰冷的</td><td>an algid winter night<br>寒冷的冬夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The algid wind off the lake numbed their faces within minutes.  湖面吹来的寒风几分钟就冻麻了他们的脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arctic</td><td>/ˈɑːrktɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极寒的；北极的</td><td>arctic temperatures<br>极寒的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An arctic wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵极寒的风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bone-chilling</td><td>/ˈboʊn ˌtʃɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷刺骨的</td><td>a bone-chilling wind<br>刺骨的寒风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bone-chilling wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵刺骨的寒风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 freezing</td><td>/ˈfriːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的，冰冻的</td><td>freezing temperatures<br>冰点以下的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited on the freezing platform for half an hour.  我们在极冷的站台上等了半小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frigid</td><td>/ˈfrɪdʒɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒的；冷漠的</td><td>a frigid stare<br>冷冰冰的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the latecomer a frigid stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地盯了迟到者一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frozen</td><td>/ˈfroʊzən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冻结的，冰冻的</td><td>a frozen lake<br>结冰的湖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children skated across the frozen pond behind the church.  孩子们在教堂后结冰的池塘上滑冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glacial</td><td>/ˈɡleɪsiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的；冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>a glacial silence<br>冰冷的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glacial tone made everyone at the table stop talking at once.  他冰冷的语气让桌边所有人立刻停止了交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ice-cold</td><td>/ˌaɪs ˈkoʊld/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的</td><td>ice-cold water<br>冰冷的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He washed his face in ice-cold water to wake himself up.  他用冰冷的水洗脸让自己清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 iced</td><td>/ˈaɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的，加冰的</td><td>an iced drink<br>加冰的饮料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ordered an iced tea and sat under the wide umbrella.  她点了一杯冰茶，坐在宽大的伞下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 icy</td><td>/ˈaɪsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的；冷淡的</td><td>an icy road<br>结冰的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave him an icy stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地瞪了他一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>swerve</b></td><td>/swɜːrv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突然改变方向</td><td>swerve to avoid<br>急转以避开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver swerved sharply to avoid a dog crossing the road.  司机猛地转向，避开横穿马路的狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detour</td><td>/ˈdiːtʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>绕道，迂路；偏离</td><td>take a long detour<br>绕一段远路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Roadworks forced us to take a long detour through the hills.  道路施工迫使我们绕道穿越丘陵走了很远。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deviate</td><td>/ˈdiːviˌeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>偏离，背离</td><td>deviate from the plan<br>偏离计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot had to deviate from the planned route because of storms.  因风暴，飞行员不得不偏离既定航线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diverge</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（使）分叉，散开；分歧</td><td>diverge from the path<br>偏离原有路径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two trails diverge just beyond the old wooden bridge.  两条小径在那座老木桥之后就分岔开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheer</td><td>/ˈʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全的，纯粹的</td><td>sheer nonsense<br>纯粹的胡说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he wrote about the treaty was sheer nonsense.  他关于那份条约写的全是胡说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swing</td><td>/ˈswɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>转向，摆动；秋千</td><td>swing sharply to the left<br>猛地转向左侧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bus swung left to avoid the fallen branch.  公交车向左急转以避开落下的树枝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veer</td><td>/vɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）转向，（使）改变航线</td><td>veer off course<br>偏离航线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to veer sharply off course in the wind.  那艘旧船在风中开始急剧偏离航线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 maintain direction</td><td>/meɪnˈteɪn dəˈrɛkʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>保持方向不变</td><td>maintain direction in fog<br>在雾中保持方向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot managed to maintain direction despite the sudden crosswind.  尽管突起侧风，飞行员仍设法保持了航向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straighten</td><td>/ˈstreɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变直，弄整齐</td><td>straighten a wire<br>把铁丝弄直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He straightened the bent nail carefully with a small hammer.  他用小锤子小心地把弯钉子弄直。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sterling</b></td><td>/ˈstɜːrlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的</td><td>a sterling performance<br>出色的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist gave a sterling performance at her first concert.  这位年轻小提琴手在首场音乐会上表现出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awesome</td><td>/ˈɑːsəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惊叹的；极好的</td><td>an awesome achievement<br>惊人的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The view from the summit was awesome enough to silence the group.  峰顶的景色壮观得让全队都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabulous</td><td>/ˈfæbjələs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幻想中的，不真实的；极好的</td><td>fabulous creatures<br>神话中的生物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps often showed fabulous creatures swimming in distant seas.  古地图上常画着在远海游动的神话生物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fantastic</td><td>/fænˈtæstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒诞的，异想天开的</td><td>a fantastic tale<br>荒诞的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fantastic story about talking wolves amused the children.  他关于会说话的狼的荒诞故事逗乐了孩子们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 first-class</td><td>/ˌfɜːrstˈklæs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一流的，最好的</td><td>a first-class education<br>一流的教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small college offers a first-class education at a modest price.  这所小学院以适中的价格提供一流的教育。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excellent</td><td>/ˈeksələnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的，极好的</td><td>excellent results<br>优异的成绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students achieved excellent results in the final exam.  学生们在期末考试中取得了优异成绩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superb</td><td>/sʊˈpɜːrb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，卓越的</td><td>a superb performance<br>一场卓越的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist gave a superb performance that night.  那晚这位年轻小提琴手献上了卓越的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superlative</td><td>/səˈpɜːrlətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最优秀的，无与匹敌的</td><td>superlative craftsmanship<br>顶级的工艺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin showed superlative craftsmanship in every carved detail.  这把小提琴在每处雕刻细节上都显出顶级工艺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wonderful</td><td>/ˈwʌndərfəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，绝妙的</td><td>a wonderful opportunity<br>极好的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Studying abroad was a wonderful opportunity she could not refuse.  出国留学是她无法拒绝的极好机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 atrocious</td><td>/əˈtroʊʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极坏的，极其残忍的</td><td>an atrocious crime<br>残暴的罪行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court described the killing as an atrocious crime.  法庭称这起杀人事件是极其残忍的罪行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 awful</td><td>/ˈɑːfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的，可怕的</td><td>an awful smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awful smell came from the forgotten lunchbox.  被遗忘的饭盒里飘出难闻的气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 execrable</td><td>/ˈeksɪkrəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的，恶劣的</td><td>execrable table manners<br>极糟糕的餐桌礼仪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel served execrable coffee at breakfast every morning.  酒店每天早餐提供的咖啡都糟糕透了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 poor</td><td>/pʊr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的，贫乏的；贫穷的</td><td>poor rocky soil<br>贫瘠的石质土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harvest was poor after a whole summer without rain.  整个夏天没下雨，收成很差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terrible</td><td>/ˈterəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的；糟糕的</td><td>a terrible storm<br>可怕的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lost everything in a terrible fire last winter.  去年冬天一场可怕的火灾让他们失去一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vile</td><td>/vaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丑陋的；（道德上）可鄙的，卑鄙的</td><td>a vile insult<br>卑劣的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shouted a vile insult and was quickly escorted outside.  他喊出一句卑劣的侮辱，很快被带了出去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wretched</td><td>/ˈretʃɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极差的；沮丧的</td><td>wretched living conditions<br>恶劣的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Families lived in wretched conditions beside the flooded riverbank.  一些家庭在泛滥的河岸边过着极差的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>swindle</b></td><td>/ˈswɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欺骗，骗取</td><td>swindle investors out of money<br>骗取投资者的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The broker swindled elderly investors out of their life savings.  经纪人骗取老年投资者的毕生积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bilk</td><td>/ˈbɪlk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>骗取，诈骗；逃避（债务）</td><td>bilk investors of millions<br>骗取投资者数百万</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bilked elderly clients out of their retirement savings.  他骗走了年长客户的退休储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 con</td><td>/ˈkɑːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，骗取；骗局</td><td>con artist<br>骗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swindler conned elderly victims out of their life savings.  那个骗子骗走了老年受害者的毕生积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hustle</td><td>/ˈhʌsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>催促；奋力行进</td><td>hustle through the crowd<br>在人群中挤行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hustled the children into the car before the rain.  她在下雨前把孩子们匆匆赶上车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiddle</td><td>/ˈfɪdəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>诈骗，做假账</td><td>fiddle the accounts<br>篡改账目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager fiddled the accounts for years before anyone noticed.  那位经理做假账多年才被人察觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gyp</td><td>/ˈdʒɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，骗取</td><td>gyp a tourist<br>骗游客的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taxi driver gypped us by taking a much longer route.  出租车司机绕了很远的路来骗我们钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bunco</td><td>/ˈbʌŋkoʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>行骗，诈骗</td><td>a bunco scheme<br>一场诈骗把戏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two men buncoed elderly residents out of their savings.  两名男子骗走了老年居民的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flimflam</td><td>/ˈflɪmˌflæm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，骗局</td><td>a clever flimflam<br>高明的骗局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole investment plan turned out to be a slick flimflam.  整个投资计划原来是个精巧的骗局。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>sybarite</b></del></td><td>/ˈsɪbəraɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>沉溺于奢侈逸乐者，酒色之徒</td><td>live like a sybarite<br>过着奢靡的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lived like a sybarite until his inheritance was completely gone.  他过着奢靡的生活，直到遗产挥霍一空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 debauchee</del></td><td>/ˌdebɔːˈʃiː/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡者，酒色之徒</td><td>an aging debauchee<br>年老的放荡者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel portrays a rich debauchee who wastes his family fortune.  这部小说描写了一个挥霍家产的富有放荡者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decadent</td><td>/ˈdekədənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，腐败的</td><td>a decadent ruling class<br>腐败堕落的统治阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the court as decadent, idle, and blind to reform.  他形容那个宫廷腐败堕落、无所作为、对改革视而不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hedonist</td><td>/ˈhiːdənɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>享乐主义者</td><td>an unashamed hedonist<br>毫不掩饰的享乐者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He calls himself a hedonist who lives only for pleasure.  他称自己是只为快乐而活的享乐主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sensualist</del></td><td>/ˈsɛnʃuəlɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>耽于声色者，酒色之徒</td><td>a notorious sensualist<br>臭名昭著的酒色之徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet was a sensualist who chased every fleeting delight.  这位诗人是个追逐一切短暂欢愉的耽于声色者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spartan</td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sycophant</b></td><td>/ˈsɪkəfænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>马屁精</td><td>surrounded by sycophants<br>被马屁精包围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king was surrounded by sycophants who praised his every word.  国王身边围着一群对他每句话都大加称赞的马屁精。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fawner</del></td><td>/ˈfɔːnər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谄媚者，献媚的人</td><td>a shameless fawner<br>无耻的谄媚者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every fawner at court praised the king's dreadful poetry.  宫中每个谄媚者都称赞国王糟糕的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flunky</td><td>/ˈflʌŋki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>马屁精，听差的仆从</td><td>the boss's flunky<br>老板的跟屁虫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sent a flunky to deliver the bad news for him.  他派了个跟屁虫替他去传坏消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lickspittle</del></td><td>/ˈlɪkˌspɪtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谄媚者，奴才</td><td>a cringing lickspittle<br>卑躬屈膝的奴才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister kept a circle of lickspittles who never disagreed.  那位大臣身边围着一群从不反对他的谄媚者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toady</td><td>/ˈtoʊdi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>马屁精；拍马屁</td><td>toady to the powerful<br>对权贵拍马屁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Junior officials toady to the powerful hoping for quick promotion.  下级官员对权贵拍马屁，希望快速升迁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apple-polisher</td><td>/ˈæpəl ˌpɑːlɪʃər/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>讨好上司的人，马屁精</td><td>an obvious apple-polisher<br>明显的马屁精</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody trusted the apple-polisher who praised every memo the boss wrote.  没人信任那个称赞老板每份备忘录的马屁精。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bootlicker</td><td>/ˈbuːtˌlɪkər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谄媚者，舔靴者</td><td>a spineless bootlicker<br>没骨气的谄媚者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His colleagues dismissed him as a bootlicker after the promotion.  升职之后同事们都把他当谄媚者看不起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 brownnoser</del></td><td>/ˈbraʊnˌnoʊzər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>拍马屁的人</td><td>a shameless brownnoser<br>无耻的马屁精</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team resented the brownnoser who reported everything to management.  团队讨厌那个把一切都报告给管理层的马屁精。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>syllabus</b></td><td>/ˈsɪləbəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>课程大纲；提纲，摘要</td><td>the course syllabus<br>课程大纲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor handed out the course syllabus on the very first day.  教授第一天上课就发了课程大纲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>syllogism</b></td><td>/ˈsɪlədʒɪzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>三段论，演绎推理</td><td>a flawed syllogism<br>有缺陷的三段论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The student's flawed syllogism led him to an obviously false conclusion.  学生有缺陷的三段论把他导向明显错误的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>symbiosis</b></td><td>/ˌsɪmbaɪˈoʊsɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>共生关系</td><td>live in symbiosis<br>共生共存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain ants and acacia trees live in a remarkable symbiosis.  某些蚂蚁与相思树处于一种奇妙的共生关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrelated growth</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈleɪtɪd ˈɡroʊθ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>互不相关的生长</td><td>unrelated growth of two species<br>两物种互不相关的生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fungus and the tree showed unrelated growth rather than true symbiosis.  真菌与树表现为互不相关的生长，而非真正的共生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>symmetry</b></td><td>/ˈsɪmətri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>对称</td><td>perfect bilateral symmetry<br>完美的左右对称</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The butterfly's wings display an almost perfect bilateral symmetry.  蝴蝶翅膀呈现近乎完美的左右对称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balance</td><td>/ˈbæləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>平衡；使平衡</td><td>keep his balance<br>保持平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer kept her balance on a single toe.  舞者用一只脚尖保持平衡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coherence</td><td>/koʊˈhɪrəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>连贯，一致性</td><td>lack of coherence<br>缺乏连贯性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay had good ideas but very little coherence between them.  这篇文章观点不错，但彼此之间缺乏连贯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonance</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐</td><td>in consonance with<br>与…相符一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her behavior was in consonance with her stated beliefs.  她的行为与她宣称的信念一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proportion</td><td>/prəˈpɔːrʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>比例，份额</td><td>a large proportion of students<br>很大比例的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A large proportion of the budget goes to teacher salaries.  预算中很大比例用于教师工资。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disproportion</td><td>/ˌdɪsprəˈpɔrʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不成比例，不均衡</td><td>a striking disproportion<br>明显的不成比例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear disproportion between the effort spent and the results.  投入的努力与结果之间明显不成比例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 asymmetry</td><td>/ˌeɪˈsɪmətri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不对称</td><td>facial asymmetry<br>面部不对称</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A slight asymmetry in the design made the building feel alive.  设计上轻微的不对称让这座建筑显得有生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discordance</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>discordance between reports<br>各报告之间的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear discordance between the two witnesses' accounts.  两位证人的叙述之间存在明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disunity</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不统一，不和</td><td>disunity within the party<br>党内的不团结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Open disunity among the leaders cost them the election.  领导层公开的不团结使他们输掉了选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imbalance</td><td>/ɪmˈbæləns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不平衡，失衡</td><td>a serious trade imbalance<br>严重的贸易失衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trade imbalance between the two countries widened again last year.  两国之间的贸易失衡去年再次扩大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incoherence</td><td>/ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不连贯，语无伦次</td><td>the incoherence of his speech<br>他讲话的不连贯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exhaustion reduced his report to complete incoherence by midnight.  到午夜时疲惫使他的报告变得毫无条理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 violence</td><td>/ˈvaɪələns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂暴，剧烈；暴力</td><td>the violence of the storm<br>暴风雨的猛烈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violence of the storm tore the harmony of the garden apart.  暴风雨的猛烈把花园的和谐撕得粉碎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>synchronous</b></td><td>/ˈsɪŋkrənəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时期的，同步的</td><td>synchronous orbit<br>同步轨道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancers' movements were perfectly synchronous throughout the piece.  舞者的动作在整支舞中完全同步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coetaneous</del></td><td>/koʊɪˈteɪniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时代的，同龄的</td><td>coetaneous manuscripts<br>同时代的手稿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two coetaneous poets never once mentioned each other in writing.  这两位同时代的诗人在作品中从未提及对方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coeval</td><td>/koʊˈiːvl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时代的，同龄的</td><td>coeval civilizations<br>同时代的文明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Those two coeval civilizations traded across the same narrow sea.  那两个同时代的文明曾在同一片狭海上通商。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coexistent</del></td><td>/ˌkoʊɪɡˈzɪstənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>共存的，同时存在的</td><td>coexistent beliefs<br>并存的信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that city several coexistent religions share the same old quarter.  在那座城市，几种共存的宗教共用同一片老城区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concurrent</td><td>/kənˈkɜːrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时发生的，并行的</td><td>concurrent sessions<br>同时进行的分会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed two concurrent sentences of three years each.  法官判处两项各三年的刑期同时执行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contemporaneous</td><td>/kənˌtempəˈreɪniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时代的，同时发生的</td><td>contemporaneous records<br>同时期的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians rely on contemporaneous letters to date the rebellion.  史学家依靠同时期的信件来确定叛乱的年代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simultaneous</td><td>/ˌsaɪməlˈteɪniəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>同时发生的，同步的</td><td>simultaneous translation<br>同声传译</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two explosions were almost simultaneous, seconds apart at most.  两次爆炸几乎同时发生，最多相隔几秒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 occurring at different times</td><td>/əˈkɜrɪŋ æt ˈdɪfrənt ˈtaɪmz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发生于不同时间的</td><td>events occurring at different times<br>发生于不同时间的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two floods, occurring at different times, were often confused.  这两次洪水发生在不同时间，却常被混为一谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 noncontemporaneous</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnkənˌtɛmpəˈreɪniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同时的，不同期的</td><td>noncontemporaneous records<br>非同期的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The layers proved noncontemporaneous, separated by thousands of years.  这些地层被证明并非同期，相隔数千年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 out-of-phase</td><td>/ˌaʊt əv ˈfeɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不同步的，相位不一致的</td><td>out-of-phase signals<br>不同步的信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two out-of-phase speakers cancelled much of the bass.  两个不同步的音箱抵消了大部分低音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>synergic</b></del></td><td>/sɪˈnɜːrdʒɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>协同的，增效的</td><td>a synergic effect<br>协同增效作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two drugs have a synergic effect far stronger than either alone.  这两种药物的协同作用远强于单独使用任何一种。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antagonistic</td><td>/ænˌtæɡəˈnɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敌对的，对抗的</td><td>antagonistic to reform<br>反对改革的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union was openly antagonistic to the new management.  工会对新管理层公开敌对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>synonymous</b></td><td>/sɪˈnɑːnɪməs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>同义的；等同于…的</td><td>synonymous with success<br>等同于成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For many readers his name became synonymous with commercial success.  对许多读者来说，他的名字成了商业成功的同义词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>synopsis</b></td><td>/sɪˈnɑːpsɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>摘要，概要</td><td>a brief synopsis<br>简短的概要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please send a brief synopsis of your novel with the application.  请随申请寄来你小说的简短概要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abstract</td><td>/æbˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>做总结，概括；使分心</td><td>abstract a long report<br>概括一份长报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abstracted the key findings into a single page.  她把主要发现概括成一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
